Triggers: "/baton", "write the baton", "make a baton", "carry-forward prompt", "next-session prompt", "continuation prompt", "/handoff-prompt". MASTER BATON (/master-baton): macro orientation doc for arcs spanning many sessions, compactions, repos, or gates.
